### 2026-10-06T00:38:28Z [**Fission-AI/OpenSpec**](https://github.com/Fission-AI/OpenSpec)

```
## What's New in v1.14.1  This release starts the CLI faster, keeps archived changes off the `openspec view` dashboard, and makes spec writing, verify, and archive more reliable.  ### Improved  - **Faster startup** - Each command loads only the code it needs, so `openspec --version` and `--help` load 24 modules instead of 485. The difference is largest on Windows. - **Spec writing** - Agents write each capability's spec file as soon as it is drafted instead of planning every file first, so long runs show progress and an interrupted run keeps the files it wrote. `/opsx:continue` writes any specs still missing before moving on. - **Strict requirement length** - A requirement over 500 characters is now a warning, so `openspec validate --strict` fails on it, including new requirements in a change.  ### Fixed  - **`openspec view`** - The dashboard no longer lists or counts archived changes. `openspec list --archived` still shows them. - **Verify with custom schemas** - `/opsx:verify` finds spec and design artifacts by their output path, so it works when a schema names those artifacts differently. - **Apply completion** - When every tracked task is checked, apply says the tasks are complete and asks you to review before archiving, instead of calling the change ready to archive. - **Archive cleanup errors** - When cleanup after a capability retirement fails, the error says the change was archived, and `--json` reports `archive_retirement_cleanup_failed`.  ## New Contributors  * @drakeo338 made their first contribution in https://github.com/Fission-AI/OpenSpec/pull/2028 * @vyhuholl made their first contribution in https://github.com/Fission-AI/OpenSpec/pull/2037  **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/Fission-AI/OpenSpec/compare/v1.14.0...v1.14.1 
```

### 2026-10-02T06:02:54Z [**Gaurav-Gosain/tuios**](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios)

```
tuios 0.8.5 makes tiling more configurable and lets herdr plugins run in a tuios pane. It adds a tmux-style size policy for sessions with more than one client. Scrollback keeps its text when you resize a pane. The sidebar files list follows the disk and panes on other machines.  Before you upgrade, read [Breaking changes and migration](#breaking-changes-and-migration). The pure Go emulator now reflows lines when a pane changes width.  The full list is at [tuios.dev/releases](https://tuios.dev/releases).  ## Install  bash brew install tuios                     # Homebrew (macOS, Linux) yay -S tuios-bin                       # AUR (tuios-web-bin for the web server) nix run github:Gaurav-Gosain/tuios     # Nix curl -fsSL https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/main/install.sh | bash go install github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/cmd/tuios@v0.8.5 docker run -it --rm ghcr.io/gaurav-gosain/tuios:v0.8.5   A binary installed by the script or from an archive updates with `tuios update`.  ## New features  - **Master position.** The master pane can be on the left, right, top or bottom, or in the center ([#325](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/325)). With `center`, the other panes go to the right and to the left in turn. This closes [#321](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/issues/321).   - `appearance.master_position` sets the side. `appearance.master_count` sets how many master panes there are (1 to 9).   - In the layout prefix (`Ctrl+B L`):     - `o` moves the master to the next side.     - `Enter` swaps the focused pane with the master.     - `m` focuses the master.     - `i` and `d` add and remove a master.   - `tuios set-layout --master-position center --masters 2` changes the current workspace.   - Each workspace keeps its own side and count, also after a daemon restart. - **herdr plugins.** In a pane, herdr's command line now answers through tuios ([#328](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/328)).   - terminal-browser, terminal-code and the Vim navigation plugins work in a tuios pane.   - The herdr socket answers 8 more methods: `pane.process_info`, `pane.focus_direction`, `pane.neighbor`, `pane.edges`, `pane.swap`, `pane.zoom`, `workspace.focus` and `agent.start`.   - Every pane gets `HERDR_TAB_ID`.   - Each method is held to the grants of the pane that calls it. `pane.process_info` shows the command line and folder only to a pane that has `write`. - **Session size.** `[daemon] window_size` sets the size of a session that has more than one client ([#330](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/330)). The values are the values of tmux's `window-size` option.   - `smallest` (the default) works as before.   - `largest` uses the biggest client.   - `latest` follows the client you typed in last.   - A client smaller than the session shows the part of the session around the cursor. A mark on the line above the dock says so.   - `tuios set-config daemon.window_size latest -s NAME` changes a running session.   - A read-only `tuios-web` viewer does not set the size under `largest` or `latest`. - **Subagents.** A Claude Code agent's rail row shows how many subagents it runs, for example `3 subagents` ([#307](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/307)). Thanks to @JakeChop.  ## Fixes  - **Scrollback and resize.** Text no longer gets lost when a pane changes size ([#331](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/331), [#333](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/333), [#323](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/323)).   - A narrower pane keeps the end of each line, and a wider pane shows it again.   - A shorter pane keeps the text below the cursor and the prompt under a full-screen program.   - A scroll region moves its rows to history.   - Wide characters stay in history.   - A daemon restart keeps history rows that are wider than the pane. - **Files list.** The sidebar files list now updates when a file is added, removed or renamed ([#319](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/319), [#327](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/327)). This closes [#313](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/issues/313).   - A pane on another machine updates its list when you `cd`, and when files change on that machine.   - In a pane that runs `ssh`, the files section says that the shell is on the other machine.   - Nothing polls. An idle session stays idle. - **New windows in BSP.** A new window now splits the focused pane, on the preselected side when you set one ([#349](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/349)). Before, it always split the last pane, which is the bottom-right one. This answers discussion [#347](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/discussions/347).   - A preselection now works in daemon sessions too.   - `longest_side` reads the size of the pane it splits. - **Zoom in the scrolling layout.** Moving to the next column while zoomed no longer shows a gap during the slide ([#324](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/324)). - **Log viewer.** The log viewer opens on the newest entry and scrolls to the last entry it draws ([#329](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/329)). Thanks to @davidreuss. - **install.sh on the BSDs.** `install.sh` downloads the right archive on FreeBSD and OpenBSD ([#322](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/322)). Thanks to @fdoving. - **herdr events.** A client that subscribes and then acts at once now gets the event ([#326](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/326)). - **Kitty images.** An image in a pane started with `new-window -- prog` no longer disappears when the session state syncs ([#343](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/343)). - **Speed.**   - The terminal emulator reads kitty and sixel image data about 15 times faster ([#338](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/338)).   - A cropped pane draws about three times faster, and a folder that changes often causes fewer sidebar redraws ([#335](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/335)).  ## Breaking changes and migration  ### Reflow  - The pure Go emulator now rewraps the lines on the screen when a pane changes width, as kitty, ghostty and tmux do. Older history keeps the width it was written at. - On the pure Go emulator, a full-width prompt that is not marked with OSC 133 leaves one extra row each time the pane gets narrower. The ghostty emulator already did this. To stop it, mark the prompt with OSC 133. See the shell recipes in `tuios doctor`.  ### herdr  - `HERDR_BIN_PATH` in a pane now names a link to tuios that answers herdr's command line. Before, it named the tuios binary. - A herdr plugin that calls `$HERDR_BIN_PATH` now reaches tuios. To run the real herdr inside a tuios pane, set `[agents] herdr_protocol = "agents"` as before.  ### Layout keys  - `o`, `Enter`, `m`, `i` and `d` in the layout prefix are new defaults. If your `[keybindings.layout_prefix]` already binds one of these keys, your binding wins.  ### Claude Code subagents  - The subagent count needs version 3 of the Claude Code integration. Run `tuios integration status`. If it reports the integration as out of date, run:    bash   tuios integration install claude-code     ### Mixed versions  - The session size policy, the herdr command front, the files list for panes on other machines, the subagent count and master positions need a v0.8.5 daemon. To start a v0.8.5 daemon, run `tuios kill-server` after the upgrade. This closes every pane in every session, so save your work first. - An older client in a session under `latest` or `largest` makes the session use `smallest` until it leaves.  ## Contributors  Thank you to everyone who sent a pull request for this release:  - @JakeChop: subagent count on the rail ([#307](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/307)) - @davidreuss: log viewer scroll ([#329](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/329)) - @fdoving: install.sh on FreeBSD and OpenBSD ([#322](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/pull/322))  Thanks to @adamcstephens for the master position request ([#321](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/issues/321)), and to @LovesAsuna for the files list reports ([#313](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/issues/313)) and the BSP report ([#347](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/discussions/347)).  ---  **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios/compare/v0.8.4...v0.8.5 
```

### 2026-10-05T02:36:42Z [**longbridge/gpui-kit**](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit)

```
# GPUI Kit v0.7.1  GPUI Kit v0.7.1 adds speech input and ColorSelect, and gives charts an initial draw-in animation. It improves IME editing, inline-token interactions, Markdown source mapping and streaming, keyboard focus, and table rendering. This release pins the GPUI snapshot dependencies to 0.3.8.  ## New components and charts  - **Speech input**: enable the `speech` feature to capture audio with `SpeechState`, start and stop sessions with `SpeechButton`, and display levels with `SpeechWaveform`. Applications can supply a `SpeechRecognizer` and `AudioInput`; native system recognition is available on supported macOS and Windows configurations, while Linux needs an application recognizer. ([#3333](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3333)) - **Speech waveform**: levels are sampled once per 80 ms, and the waveform starts empty and scrolls smoothly from the trailing edge using its own playhead. ([#3352](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3352)) - **ColorSelect**: use a framed color field with a swatch, hex value and caret in forms. `ColorSelect::new` shares `ColorPickerState` with the compact picker, and supports placeholder and accessible-name overrides. `ColorPicker` now applies its root style refinements. ([#3289](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3289)) - **Chart appear motion**: built-in charts draw their data in on first paint, with reduced-motion support. Use `.appear(false)` to opt out or `.appear_key(...)` to replay; `PlotAppearScope` remembers completed animations across virtual-list remounts while the scope remains painted. Existing custom plots retain their behavior unless they opt in. ([#3296](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3296), [#3308](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3308))  ## Editor and text input  - **Inline-token hover**: `Input::on_token_hover` and `Textarea::on_token_hover` report token entry and exit with `InlineTokenHoverEvent`, so applications can anchor tooltips or previews without changing selection. JavaScript bindings expose the same interaction. ([#3346](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3346)) - Fixed IME candidate positioning before repaint and grouped rewrites of a typed character, including Korean IME input, into the typing undo step. ([#3297](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3297), [#3341](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3341)) - Fixed Textarea row-based height, text clipping in short single-line frames, and consistency between soft wrapping and horizontal scrolling. ([#3330](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3330), [#3343](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3343), [#3358](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3358)) - Fixed completion-prefix measurement and stale trigger offsets, Kotlin syntax-query compilation, and stale syntax-injection ranges. ([#3363](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3363), [#3367](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3367), [#3317](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3317)) - Fixed mask validation for non-ASCII characters, including Chinese literal separators and characters matched by `*`. ([#3369](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3369))  ## Markdown and text rendering  - **Source-based highlights**: `RenderedText::source` exposes the source of the rendered snapshot, and `range_for_source` converts Markdown source byte ranges into rendered ranges for `RangeHighlight`. ([#3281](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3281)) - **Scrollable code blocks**: a code-block style with vertical scrolling and a height cap now provides its own scrollbar, handles nested wheel scrolling and keeps code actions pinned. ([#3322](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3322)) - Improved streaming through incremental prefix updates in `TextViewState::set_text` and by committing valid background parse results overtaken by newer chunks. ([#3294](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3294), [#3344](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3344)) - Fixed CJK punctuation wrapping, inherited text colors in filled containers, and ragged GFM table rows; scroll-table column measurements are reused. ([#3293](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3293), [#3329](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3329), [#3365](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3365), [#3318](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3318))  ## Dock and controls  - **Dock**: `DockArea::set_split_sizes` restores a split's proportions in place, without rebuilding panels, and reports one layout change. ([#3314](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3314)) - **Questionnaire**: choosing a single-choice answer now confirms the item and advances or submits after a brief feedback pause. `QuestionnaireState::choose` exposes that activation path; `activate_choice` continues to change the answer without confirming. Arrow-key selection, multiple-choice toggles and freeform typing do not auto-advance. ([#3350](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3350)) - **Keyboard focus**: borderless buttons show a legible focus line, DataTable shows keyboard focus, and popup menus preserve a single keyboard highlight. ([#3299](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3299), [#3300](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3300), [#3307](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3307)) - **Control sizing**: control text follows the Input/Select size ladder, OtpInput follows interface zoom, and Large Switch uses a larger track. ([#3287](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3287)) - **Clipboard**: buttons have a localized accessible name by default; `accessibility_label` provides a contextual override. ([#3354](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3354)) - **Slider**: touch drags on the track move the thumb. ([#3313](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3313)) - **Radio**: disabled controls stay disabled inside RadioGroup. ([#3331](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3331)) - **Shimmer**: the default highlight remains visible on foreground text in dark mode. ([#3328](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3328)) - **Command**: item-row corners stay concentric with the frame. ([#3285](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3285))  ## Runtime, platform and documentation  - **GPUI snapshot**: exact snapshot dependencies are updated to `=0.3.8`, with continuation-indent API adaptation preserving the previous wrapping behavior. ([#3370](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3370))  - **Web runtime**: target-specific Instant handling avoids a touch-related wasm panic. ([#3271](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3271)) - **Scrolling**: phaseless mouse wheels continue scrolling after an edge bounce. ([#3316](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3316)) - **Shell**: QuickJS JIT is updated to 0.12.11. ([#3338](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3338)) - **Kit macros**: the hidden GPUI re-export points at Kit, keeping generated paths consistent. ([#3306](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3306)) - **Documentation**: release snapshots drive onboarding dependency versions; setup documents the macOS font-kit requirement, and skill examples correct app startup, async and Select re-entrancy usage. ([#3284](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3284), [#3356](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3356), [#3339](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3339), [#3345](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3345), [#3351](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3351))  ## Behavior changes  Charts animate on first paint by default; disable this with `.appear(false)`. Non-interactive built-in charts now retain an element ID for animation and caches, while remaining non-interactive. ([#3296](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3296))  diff   let chart = LineChart::new(data).interactive(false); - assert!(Plot::id(&chart).is_none()); + assert!(Plot::id(&chart).is_some()); + assert!(!Plot::interactive(&chart));   Single-choice Questionnaire activation now confirms by default. Applications that need to change an answer without confirmation can continue to call `activate_choice`. ([#3350](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3350))  Thank you to everyone who contributed code, documentation, testing, and feedback!  ## What's Changed * command: Keep item row radius concentric with the frame by @madcodelife in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3285 * website: Follow release versions and highlight diffs by @huacnlee in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3284 * base: Gate GlobalState's Instant by target to fix a wasm touch panic by @yoyo930021 in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3271 * color_picker: Add ColorSelect, a framed field that opens the picker by @huacnlee in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3289 * text: Keep inline flow lines within the wrap width for CJK punctuation by @madcodelife in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3293 * button: Show keyboard focus on borderless variants when `focus_ring` is off by @madcodelife in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3299 * chart: Add appear motion to `Plot` and every chart by @madcodelife in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3296 * text: parse prefix extensions incrementally in TextViewState::set_text (#3291) by @Yi-111-a in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3294 * button: Keep the focus line off text and legible on fills by @madcodelife in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3300 * website: Fit StatusBar demo sections to narrow windows by @ihavecoke in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3301 * docs: Update gpui-kit dependency version to 0.7 in READMEs by @obito-t in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3304 * website: Keep box-drawing glyphs in the gallery font subsets by @ihavecoke in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3305 * table, menu: Show keyboard focus on `DataTable` and keep menu highlights on key presses by @madcodelife in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3307 * plot: Keep finished appears across remounts with `PlotAppearScope` by @madcodelife in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3308 * kit: Point the hidden gpui re-export at the Kit itself by @huacnlee in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3306 * dock: Add `DockArea::set_split_sizes` to restore a split's slot sizes by @IHadACookie in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3314 * component: Align control text sizes with Input and Select by @huacnlee in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3287 * scroll_bounce: Let phaseless wheels scroll again after bouncing at an edge by @ylinwind in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3316 * highlighter: Clip stale injection ranges to char boundaries before slicing by @huacnlee in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3317 * slider: Let a touch drag on the track move the thumb by @Bombatomica64 in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3313 * chore: upgrade notify to 8.2 and harden theme watching by @orielhaim in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3320 * shell: update QuickJS JIT to 0.12.11 by @huacnlee in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3338 * docs: Document macOS font-kit requirement by @huacnlee in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3339 * text: Avoid remeasuring scroll-table column widths by @lurenjia534 in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3318 * markdown: add source & range_for_source by @tidely in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3281 * text_view: Let fenced code blocks scroll inside a height cap by @jarviisha in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3322 * shimmer: Keep the default highlight visible on foreground text in dark mode by @Bombatomica64 in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3328 * text_view: Follow the container's text color so rich text reads in filled bubbles by @Bombatomica64 in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3329 * input: Make rows set the height of a plain Textarea again by @Bombatomica64 in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3330 * radio: Keep a * text: Commit background parses that a newer streamed chunk overtook by @FenjuFu in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3344 * input: fall back to last caret for IME candidate bounds before # Chatless 0.7.1 发布  ## 下载 ### Windows - [Chatless Setup (.exe)](https://github.com/kamjin3086/chatless/releases/download/v0.7.1/Chatless_0.7.1_x64-setup.exe) - [Chatless MSI (.msi)](https://github.com/kamjin3086/chatless/releases/download/v0.7.1/Chatless_0.7.1_x64_en-US.msi)            ### macOS - [Apple Silicon](https://github.com/kamjin3086/chatless/releases/download/v0.7.1/Chatless_0.7.1_aarch64.dmg) - [Intel 处理器（通过 Rosetta 2 运行）](https://github.com/kamjin3086/chatless/releases/download/v0.7.1/Chatless_0.7.1_aarch64.dmg)  ### Linux - [Red Hat/CentOS/Fedora (.rpm)](https://github.com/kamjin3086/chatless/releases/download/v0.7.1/Chatless-0.7.1-1.x86_64.rpm) - [Ubuntu/Debian (.deb)](https://github.com/kamjin3086/chatless/releases/download/v0.7.1/Chatless_0.7.1_amd64.deb) - [通用格式 (.AppImage)](https://github.com/kamjin3086/chatless/releases/download/v0.7.1/Chatless_0.7.1_amd64.AppImage)            ---  ## 安装说明  ### Windows 1. 下载 .exe 文件（推荐）或 .msi 文件 2. 双击运行安装程序 3. 按提示完成安装  ### macOS 1. 根据处理器选择版本：    - Apple Silicon (M1/M2/M3): 下载 aarch64.dmg    - Intel 处理器: 下载 aarch64.dmg 通过 Rosetta 2 运行 2. 双击打开，拖拽到 Applications 文件夹 3. 从 Applications 启动应用  **注意**: 首次启动可能提示"已损坏"，请： 1. 点击"取消" 2. 打开终端，运行:   3. 重新启动应用  ### Linux 1. 根据发行版选择格式：    - Red Hat/CentOS/Fedora: 下载 .rpm 文件    - Ubuntu/Debian: 下载 .deb 文件    - 其他发行版: 下载 .AppImage 文件 2. 使用包管理器安装或直接运行  ---  ## 常见问题  **Windows 闪退**: 安装 [Visual C++ 运行库](https://aka.ms/vs/17/release/vc_redist.x64.exe)  **macOS 无法打开**: 使用终端命令移除隔离属性（见上方说明）  **Linux 权限问题**: 确保给予执行权限 `chmod +x`  ---  ## 文档  - [安装指南](https://github.com/kamjin3086/chatless/blob/main/INSTALLATION_INSTRUCTIONS.md) - [项目主页](https://github.com/kamjin3086/chatless) - [问题反馈](https://github.com/kamjin3086/chatless/issues)  ---  ## 更新内容  ### 修复 - 修复 0.7.0 启动后卡在"正在初始化模型服务"、窗口无法拖动、鼠标显示繁忙图标的问题。原因是 0.7.0 新增的窗口圆角逻辑在窗口事件回调里调用了 Tauri 的窗口查询接口，等于在事件循环内部再向事件循环投递消息并等待返回，形成持续的消息循环把主线程占满（进程 100% CPU），前端初始化因此永远无法完成。现在改为直接向 AppKit 查询窗口是否最大化/全屏，不再回调 Tauri。 
```

### 2026-10-04T13:40:03Z [**gildas-lormeau/SingleFile**](https://github.com/gildas-lormeau/SingleFile)

```
### Changes  - single-file-core is updated from 1.6.22 to 1.6.24, see https://github.com/gildas-lormeau/single-file-core/releases/tag/v1.6.23 and https://github.com/gildas-lormeau/single-file-core/releases/tag/v1.6.24 (fonts declared in nested rules, `font-width` and `tech()` sources, fonts created by scripts, specificity per spec, zip.js 2.23.0) - The privacy policy mentions the WebDAV, S3 and REST form API destinations  ### Fixes  - With "remove unused fonts", a font named only in a nested style rule was removed, and the text fell back to another font - A `url()` in `@scope`, `@starting-style`, `@keyframes` or `@page` was not saved in the page - A font created by a page script with `new FontFace(...)` could be saved with an invalid family name - Removing a script, a `<noscript>`, a resource hint or a hidden input from the body shifted the position of the elements after it, so `:nth-child()` and similar selectors matched other elements - In Firefox, a stylesheet imported by a `<style>` element was applied empty in a self-extracting archive, so pages loading Google Fonts this way lost their fonts 
```

manager iterator annotations (`UP052`) ([#29000](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/29000))  ### Performance  - Reduce memory used by diagnostics ([#28951](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/28951))  ### Server  - Avoid running `uv format` in untrusted workspaces ([#28873](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/28873))  ### Documentation  - Fix links to moved changelog sections and renamed mdtests ([#28941](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/28941)) - Add Python 3.15 as a supported version ([#28907](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/28907)) - Add ty as a type checker example ([#28906](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/28906))  ### Other changes  - Update Rust toolchain to 1.99 and MSRV to 1.97 ([#29047](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/29047))  ### Contributors  - [@ntBre](https://github.com/ntBre) - [@spaceone](https://github.com/spaceone) - ### 2026-10-05T23:40:54Z [**dani-garcia/vaultwarden**](https://github.com/dani-garcia/vaultwarden)

```
## Security Fixes This release contains security fixes for the following advisories. We strongly advise updating as soon as possible. - Organization member revocation [[GHSA-69q9-v8p6-xvx3]](https://github.com/dani-garcia/vaultwarden/security/advisories/GHSA-69q9-v8p6-xvx3) (**High**, 8.1) - Two-factor authentication [[GHSA-7jg8-8m5x-6j9r]](https://github.com/dani-garcia/vaultwarden/security/advisories/GHSA-7jg8-8m5x-6j9r) (**Medium**, 6.8) - Organization invitations [[GHSA-v576-3wvq-xh3c]](https://github.com/dani-garcia/vaultwarden/security/advisories/GHSA-v576-3wvq-xh3c) (**Medium**, 6.8) - Attachments [[GHSA-q5x6-grh5-fqgc]](https://github.com/dani-garcia/vaultwarden/security/advisories/GHSA-q5x6-grh5-fqgc) (**Medium**, 6.5) - Organization event logs [[GHSA-64mc-4p6f-r7x9]](https://github.com/dani-garcia/vaultwarden/security/advisories/GHSA-64mc-4p6f-r7x9) (**Medium**, 4.3) - Cipher sharing [[GHSA-7ccc-c43j-4p36]](https://github.com/dani-garcia/vaultwarden/security/advisories/GHSA-7ccc-c43j-4p36) (**Medium**, 4.3) - Organization API key [[GHSA-qwx4-wcv4-mpcv]](https://github.com/dani-garcia/vaultwarden/security/advisories/GHSA-qwx4-wcv4-mpcv) (**Low**, 3.8) - Additional dependency updates and minor security enhancements  These are private for now, pending CVE assignment and publishing at a later date.  > [!NOTE] > If an organization has Admins you don't fully trust, consider rotating its API key after updating (Admin Console → Settings → Rotate API key). Before this release, Admins could also view the key.  ## Upgrade notes - **Reverse proxies:** with `IP_HEADER=X-Forwarded-For`, the client IP is now the rightmost address that isn't in `IP_HEADER_TRUSTED_PROXIES` (it used to be the leftmost). If you have several proxies in a row, for example a CDN in front of nginx, add all of them to `IP_HEADER_TRUSTED_PROXIES`. Otherwise the address of the proxy in front is used for rate limiting and logs. - **Sends:** `bw send receive` on CLI 2026.4.2 and older no longer works, the same as against Bitwarden's own servers since v2026.8.0. Creating and managing Sends works on all clients. - **Feature flags:** these flags were removed because no client reads them anymore: `ssh-agent`, `ssh-key-vault-item`, `mutual-tls`, `anon-addy-self-host-alias`, `simple-login-self-host-alias`, `pm-25373-windows-biometrics-v2`, `pm-26340-linux-biometrics-v2`, `desktop-ui-migration-milestone-1` to `-4`, `cxp-import-mobile` and `cxp-export-mobile`. If `EXPERIMENTAL_CLIENT_FEATURE_FLAGS` still lists one of them, startup logs a warning and saving settings in the admin panel fails until it's removed. - **Duo:** `DUO_USE_IFRAME` (the deprecated Traditional Prompt) is removed and ignored if set. - **Custom templates:** there's a new email template, `email/recover_twofactor`, sent after a login with a two-step recovery code. - The legacy `POST /identity/accounts/register` and `POST /api/accounts/prelogin` endpoints are removed. No current client uses them. - When using a MariaDB or MySQL server which does not support native TLS/SSL encrypted connections, using the Alpine image of Vaultwarden might fail to connect. Add `MARIADB_TLS_DISABLE_PEER_VERIFICATION=1` as an environment variable to your Vaultwarden server environment, and it will start running again.  ## What's Changed * [Web 2026.9.0] Support the vault banner policy by @tom27052006 in https://github.com/dani-garcia/vaultwarden/pull/7748 * Add support for basic auth response client feature flag by @tom27052006 in https://github.com/dani-garcia/vaultwarden/pull/7745 * [web-v2026.8.1] store the user key ID by @Timshel in https://github.com/dani-garcia/vaultwarden/pull/7693 * Add organizationsNew and policiesNew to sync response by @tom27052006 in https://github.com/dani-garcia/vaultwarden/pull/7666 * Add `pm-32009-new-item-types` feature flag by @bdd in https://github.com/dani-garcia/vaultwarden/pull/7478 * Update Crates, GHA and JS by @BlackDex in https://github.com/dani-garcia/vaultwarden/pull/7751 * Add `pm-34171-card-scanner` feature flag by @bdd in https://github.com/dani-garcia/vaultwarden/pull/7477 * set user_created bool for each separate invitation by @stefan0xC in https://github.com/dani-garcia/vaultwarden/pull/7753 * Fix revoked org members retaining access to org ciphers by @abhiShandy in https://github.com/dani-garcia/vaultwarden/pull/7554 * Ensure all user checked routes are confirmed by @dani-garcia in https://github.com/dani-garcia/vaultwarden/pull/7763 * Fix cortex-a53 build issues when using xx-cargo by @BlackDex in https://github.com/dani-garcia/vaultwarden/pull/7774 * Add `undetermined-cipher-scenario-logic` feature flag (closes #7801) by @cad0p in https://github.com/dani-garcia/vaultwarden/pull/7802 * Add Windows native credential sync to supported feature flags by @KingIronMan2011 in https://github.com/dani-garcia/vaultwarden/pull/7798 * Hide the whole change-email section when EMAIL_CHANGE_ALLOWED is false by @tom27052006 in https://github.com/dani-garcia/vaultwarden/pull/7759 * Fix Clippy warnings across all targets by @tom27052006 in https://github.com/dani-garcia/vaultwarden/pull/7782 * Admin reset: 2fa email fallback need a verified email by @Timshel in https://github.com/dani-garcia/vaultwarden/pull/7770 * Sends cleanup: remove legacy endpoints and align with upstream by @dani-garcia in https://github.com/dani-garcia/vaultwarden/pull/7806 * Remove legacy API endpoints and compatibility code by @dani-garcia in https://github.com/dani-garcia/vaultwarden/pull/7809 * Align API with upstream and remove unwraps by @dani-garcia in https://github.com/dani-garcia/vaultwarden/pull/7810 * Update crates, Rust and other dependencies by @BlackDex in https://github.com/dani-garcia/vaultwarden/pull/7814  ## New Contributors * @bdd made their first contribution in https://github.com/dani-garcia/vaultwarden/pull/7478 * @abhiShandy made their first contribution in https://github.com/dani-garcia/vaultwarden/pull/7554 * @cad0p made their first contribution in https://github.com/dani-garcia/vaultwarden/pull/7802 * @KingIronMan2011 made their first contribution in https://github.com/dani-garcia/vaultwarden/pull/7798  **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/dani-garcia/vaultwarden/compare/1.37.3...1.37.4   [Join Discussion](https://github.com/dani-garcia/vaultwarden/discussions/7823) 
```

### 2026-10-05T08:09:36Z [**ryan4yin/nix-config**](https://github.com/ryan4yin/nix-config)

```
## Highlights  Four months of work across the desktops, the homelab VMs, and the self-hosted services:  - **Desktop**: Noctalia v5 shell, Zed replaces VS Code, native mihomo replaces Clash Verge,   and voice input via `fcitx5-vinput` (CPU/Sherpa-ONNX) on `ai` and `shoukei`. - **Virtualization**: home services moved from KubeVirt to libvirt/microVMs, the three VM hosts   renamed to `12kingdoms-*`, and the `k3s-test-1` control planes now run as microVMs. - **Services**: RustFS replaces MinIO for S3, plus Immich and Jellyfin on `youko`. - **Security & backup**: default-deny inbound firewall (LAN + tailnet trusted), expanded AppArmor   hardening, and btrbk/restic backups with failure notifications. - **Agents & docs**: substantial global rule and repository documentation updates.  ## What's Changed * feat: replace vscode with zed-editor by @ryan4yin in https://github.com/ryan4yin/nix-config/pull/263 * feat(nixos): manage scattered trash dirs with trash-cli retention timer by @ryan4yin in https://github.com/ryan4yin/nix-config/pull/265 * security: hardening pass 1 (AppArmor, firewall default, loopback metrics, k3s perms, SSH X11) by @ryan4yin in https://github.com/ryan4yin/nix-config/pull/266 * fix(darwin): remove unused aerospace configuration by @ryan4yin in https://github.com/ryan4yin/nix-config/pull/267 * fix(sunshine): use Intel rendering and recover remote Niri sessions by @ryan4yin in https://github.com/ryan4yin/nix-config/pull/268 * docs(agents): add SRE change guardrails and refine global rules by @ryan4yin in https://github.com/ryan4yin/nix-config/pull/269 * Add open agent tools and tighten privacy defaults by @ryan4yin in https://github.com/ryan4yin/nix-config/pull/270 * feat(btrbk): make snapshots explicit, guarded, and stored in @snapshots by @ryan4yin in https://github.com/ryan4yin/nix-config/pull/271 * fix: resolve audited system component bugs by @ryan4yin in https://github.com/ryan4yin/nix-config/pull/272 * feat(noctalia): upgrade to the native v5 shell by @ryan4yin in https://github.com/ryan4yin/nix-config/pull/273 * fix(nixpak): repair sandboxed audio and deduplicate mounts by @ryan4yin in https://github.com/ryan4yin/nix-config/pull/274 * feat(kubevirt): Linux bridge + Cilium instead of OVS/flannel by @ryan4yin in https://github.com/ryan4yin/nix-config/pull/278 * fix(victoriametrics): enable dedup to accept duplicate samples by @ryan4yin in https://github.com/ryan4yin/nix-config/pull/279 * refactor(kubevirt): memory tuning - drop static hugepages, add zram + earlyoom by @ryan4yin in https://github.com/ryan4yin/nix-config/pull/280 * fix(kubevirt): use the correct earlyoom option name by @ryan4yin in https://github.com/ryan4yin/nix-config/pull/281 * feat(kubevirt-youko): serve VM golden images from a shared NFS store by @ryan4yin in https://github.com/ryan4yin/nix-config/pull/282 * chore: remove the never-deployed k3s-prod-1 cluster by @ryan4yin in https://github.com/ryan4yin/nix-config/pull/284 * feat(k3s-test-1): add three worker nodes and taint the control plane by @ryan4yin in https://github.com/ryan4yin/nix-config/pull/283 * feat(upload-vm): write the image into the VM's own disk by @ryan4yin in https://github.com/ryan4yin/nix-config/pull/285 * docs(k8s): note the flaky USB bridge and the aquamarine microVM plan by @ryan4yin in https://github.com/ryan4yin/nix-config/pull/286 * feat(vms): migrate the idol desktops from KubeVirt to libvirt by @ryan4yin in https://github.com/ryan4yin/nix-config/pull/287 * feat: run aquamarine's services natively on kubevirt-youko by @ryan4yin in https://github.com/ryan4yin/nix-config/pull/288 * feat(microvm): run the k3s-test-1 masters as microVMs by @ryan4yin in https://github.com/ryan4yin/nix-config/pull/289 * refactor: rename the KubeVirt hardware config to qemu-guest by @ryan4yin in https://github.com/ryan4yin/nix-config/pull/290 * chore(hosts): retire the k3s cluster on the physical hosts by @ryan4yin in https://github.com/ryan4yin/nix-config/pull/291 * refactor: rename the  ## 📦 升级指南  ### 镜像更新  - 更新 fastgpt-app（FastGPT 主服务）镜像 tag: v4.17.1 - 更新 fastgpt-pro（FastGPT 商业版）镜像 tag: v4.17.1 - 更新 fastgpt-plugin 镜像 tag: v1.1.4 - 更新 AI Proxy 镜像 tag: v0.7.3  ### 升级脚本  本次升级包含以下自动化脚本  1. 回填应用资源权限快照，清理遗留 V1 应用及版本数据，并校验应用正式版本；迁移任务不阻塞服务启动，可在系统升级任务中查看进度和失败记录。 2. 为兼容渠道启用 reasoning 映射：为现有 AIProxy OpenAI 协议渠道开启 `map_reasoning_to_reasoning_content: true` 的响应字段转换，解决深度思考模型（如 DeepSeek-R1）的思考内容字段兼容问题，并保留渠道的原有配置；已配置该项的渠道不会重复更新，迁移任务不阻塞服务启动。 3. 升级知识库标签数据：将存量 Collection 标签迁移为类型化标签，并清理重复标签定义；迁移任务不阻塞服务启动。 4. 回填团队成员名设置标记：为历史团队成员回填 `isSetMemberName` 标记；owner 始终标记为已设置，非 owner 中与登录用户名相同的成员名标记为需要补充；迁移任务不阻塞服务启动。  ## 管理员入口调整  商业版管理界面已并入 FastGPT 主应用，root 用户登录后可从「管理员」入口访问系统配置、运营管理和 License 激活、续期页面。系统配置位于 `/admin/settings/*`，系统工具和系统模型分别位于 `/admin/resources/tool` 和 `/admin/resources/model`。旧路径不再保留。  FastGPT Pro 服务仍需保留；若为 Pro 配置了自定义健康检查，请将探测路径调整为 `/api/health`。  ## 🚀 新增内容  1. 应用发布版本新增资源权限快照和运行时授权，静态资源按发布时快照校验，动态资源按实际运行用户校验。解决协作开发的应用，运行时权限分散导致无法正确计算问题。 2. 新增知识库标签管理与筛选，支持类型化标签值、集合标签维护，以及在工作流和 Agent V2 知识库搜索中使用标签条件。 3. 修改密码支持第三方认证，并统一改密授权、验证和密码更新流程。 4. Agent V2 的知识库搜索支持知识库引用，聊天中可展示本次检索实际返回的引用内容。 5. 应用增加 Token 统计看板，用量记录同步展示 Token 消耗。 6. App、Agent、Skill、工具和 Dataset 列表支持批量选择、移动和删除，并根据管理权限限制可操作资源。 7. 管理员功能统一集成到主应用，支持按 License 授权展示管理菜单和功能状态。 8. 文件读取节点调试支持本地上传文件和添加文件链接，并在上传未完成或文件异常时阻止运行。 9. 新增知识库文件级权限（集合级权限）：可按文件/文件夹单独配置协作者，并支持继承与独立态、恢复继承、所有权转移。开关默认关闭，存量知识库行为不变；开启后列表、详情与检索召回均按文件权限逐条鉴权，关闭则清空全部文件权限配置。详见 [知识库文件级权限](../../../guide/dataset/collection_permission)。 10. 系统模型健康状态探测与监控：管理员支持开启系统模型健康状态定时探测，提供 48 小时状态图可视化监控，并支持配置告警 Webhook 和 Token，在模型状态发生变动时触发 Webhook 通知。详见 [系统模型配置与监控](../../config/model/intro)。 11. 首次登录待办与引导流程：首次登录支持强制要求补齐团队成员显示姓名，自动提示并处理待确认的团队邀请，并统一编排系统公告与企业认证提示等后续弹窗。 12. 系统模型标识符修改：管理员支持通过稳定的 `modelId` 直接修改系统模型的模型标识符（`model`），并自动同步更新关联渠道的模型映射与列表。 13. 支持 OFD 文件解析。 14. 新增词元跳动 (TokenDance) 统一 AI API 网关接入文档与示例。 15. 新增 SSO 密码策略：管理员可禁止 SSO 命名空间账号使用平台密码登录和修改密码，登录页会按可用的身份验证方式展示入口。 16. 个人工作流工具支持配置跟随最新发布版本，应用更新后无需重新手动调整工具版本。 17. 工作流新增 `off` 节点输入类型：专门面向第三方接口传参，界面不渲染该输入，但完整保留参数定义、默认值与校验，且禁止作为 Agent 自动生成参数。 18. 工作流知识库搜索支持字符串（`string`）类型标签筛选：支持结构化文本和引用变量过滤，并在检索层执行对应匹配逻辑。 19. OpenAPI 开放文档新增「基础功能 - 模型管理」分类与接口，开放公开模型列表、成员可用模型清单和批量模型详情接口，支持通过 API Key 查询当前可用模型。 20. 新增环境变量 `DISABLE_MARKETPLACE`（默认为 `false`）：支持隐藏系统工具中的插件市场安装入口，仅保留本地文件导入。 21. 管理员创建用户接口支持显式传入并保存成员显示名称（`memberName`）。 22. 知识库、技能、应用都增加被使用引用，便于看到该资源被哪些应用引用。  ## ⚙️ 优化  1. 调整知识库集合批量操作栏、标签间距和标签管理空状态。 2. App 日志使用者筛选改为打开筛选框后再加载，支持服务端搜索和分页，移除固定的 100 个使用者限制。 3. 知识库搜索节点支持记录重排前的召回候选集，便于对比检索与重排结果；通过 `RETRIEVAL_RESULTS_LIMIT` 配置记录上限，默认不记录。 4. 模型选择器将 Beta 模型排在列表末尾。 5. 知识库默认图片模型清空后被自动回填的问题，允许不配置图片模型。 6. 重构管理员系统配置表单交互与布局设计，提供更清晰的分类导航与配置保存体验。 7. 商业版 License 激活支持永久/无到期时间（unlimited expired time），完善无到期时间许可证的显示与校验。 8. 优化中文语言环境下的大数字格式化，正确展示万、亿等本土化单位。 9. 表格默认开启横向滚动并移除多余左右内边距，长表格在窄屏下更易浏览。 10. 工作流数组类型变量收到单个对象的 JSON 字符串时被解析为普通对象、导致循环与并行节点报错「不是数组」的问题，自动将其包装为单元素数组。 11. 从模板添加模型列表支持按模型提供商（Provider）预设顺序排序返回，保持界面模型分组展示整齐一致。 12. 优化会话标题生成规则：首轮仅上传文件或触发自动执行时支持生成本地化固定标题（如「上传文件」/「自动执行」），后续轮次输入提问文本后自动以新问题标题覆盖；用户自定义标题（`customTitle`）拥有最高优先级且不会被自动覆盖；统一会话历史、日志与导出等处的标题展示与兜底逻辑，并禁止将空值或占位符持久化为标题。 13. 优化多模态消息 Token 估算：多模态内容（图片、音频、视频、文件）在进入 Token 计算 Worker 前使用固定占位符替代 base64、媒体 URL 或二进制数据，避免长 URL 或大二进制数据导致 Token 计算超时或估算严重偏离。 14. 优化找回密码验证流程：在发送验证码前校验输入的账号是否符合邮箱或手机号格式，避免非法账号在打开图形验证码或请求接口时提示参数校验错误。  ## 🐛 修复  1. 工具调用隔离工作流工具运行时状态，并恢复交互节点记忆，避免并发调用相互污染或丢失交互结果。 2. 修复发票抬头表单回填时覆盖现有输入、必填字段校验不完整的问题。 3. 临时对话尚未保存到历史记录时不再显示自定义标题操作，避免修改结果无法保存。 4. 修复 FastGPT Pro 登录后的许可证校验流程，并补充账单相关接口的鉴权校验。 5. 修复同一聊天轮次内并发写入人类消息和 AI 消息导致 MongoDB 事务报错的问题。 6. 修复解析文档中的 HTML 图片无法预览的问题，支持将 HTML 标签内的 S3 对象键转换为预览地址。 7. 修复文本清理误删正文中的 `]]`，并修复中文字符之间连续空白未全部清理的问题。 8. 修复提示词编辑器中的变量和 Skill 标签选择、复制及粘贴异常。 9. 修复模型生成 JSON 后追加包含括号的说明文字时，JSON 提取范围错误的问题。 10. 修复 PowerPoint 段落中的软换行被丢弃、导致相邻文本粘连的问题。 11. 修复表格单元格中的反斜杠和 HTML 无表头表格转换为 Markdown 时破坏表格结构的问题。 12. 工具执行失败不再被当作普通聊天错误展示在底部错误卡片中，也不再计入节点响应摘要的错误数。 13. 修复应用聊天测试未提前校验模型可用性的问题，在发送请求前提示具体的模型配置错误。 14. 修复 FastGPT Pro 飞书发布渠道配置 Encrypt Key 时，事件订阅 URL 校验无法正确返回 challenge 的问题。 15. 修复工具切回手动输入时错误显示 JSON 编辑器的问题，沿用节点声明的输入控件。 16. 修复 MongoDB 变更监听终止后无法恢复的问题，增加自动重连及配置重新加载。 17. 修复系统配置并发更新引发的 MongoDB 事务错误。 18. 修复工作流中未激活或未开启思考的模型节点错误展示空白思考输出卡片的问题。 19. 修复 HTML 表格转换为 Markdown 时未识别 `<th>` 单元格，导致标准 HTML 表格丢掉列名的问题。 20. 修复子工作流未发布节点调试详情时，父工作流流式响应可能异常中断的问题。 21. 修复 iOS Safari 浏览器在异步请求后打开知识库集合源文件链接被弹窗拦截的问题。 22. 修复为成员分配协作者角色时未严格遵循群组与组织管理员权限的问题。 23. 修复工作流单节点调试时连线状态被错误重置为 waiting，导致接口立即返回且前端一直处于运行中状态的问题。 24. 修复知识库切块处理带括号的 Markdown 图片 URL 时截断地址，导致图片向量化或描述关联错误的问题。 25. 修复空文件和 Office 临时锁定文件上传校验异常的问题，并统一知识库图片上传的内容校验。 26. 修复 MCP DNS rebinding 导致的 SSRF 风险，并收紧解析地址族校验。 27. 移除远程图片的 HEAD 预校验，避免恶意地址造成请求阻塞或拒绝服务。 28. 修复聊天记录详情中错误显示「内容由 AI 生成」提示的问题。 29. 修复 JSON Schema 属性使用布尔值 `required` 时无法正确识别必填项的问题。 30. 修复 OpenAPI Scalar 同源测试请求未正确识别为 Web 请求，导致 CSRF 校验失败的问题。 31. 修复外链删除权限判断和前端操作反馈，并在删除应用后立即清理外链与快捷引用。 32. 修复表格读取长整数时被转换为科学计数法或丢失精度的问题。 33. 修复严格枚举参数忽略 JSON Schema `default` 值、始终选择第一个枚举项的问题。 34. 修复工作流与 Agent 运行中丢弃中间工具调用（ToolCall）Assistant 消息与响应顺序错乱的问题，确保流式输出与持久化时完整保留工具调用转录与节点响应。 35. 修复子工作流和交互式工具暂停恢复时的状态丢失问题，恢复执行时正确传递当前提问与用户输入上下文。 36. 修复 Markdown 渲染未过滤危险链接协议（如 `javascript:`、`vbscript:` 等）导致的存储型点击 XSS 安全风险。 37. 修复知识库检索结果重排融合（RRF）时直接修改输入列表 score 对象的问题，确保重排前记录的召回候选集分数保持真实原始值。 38. 修复通用并发任务调度器 `batchRun` 中任一任务失败后其他并发槽继续领取新任务的问题，报错后立即终止新任务分配。 39. 修复启用 `CHECK_INTERNAL_IP=true` 内网防御时，聊天日志推送、钉钉 Token 获取、第三方 API 知识库同步与自定义 PDF 解析等系统配置的内网服务被误拦截的问题。 40. 修复应用资源回填迁移中遗留 V1 应用及包含非 ObjectId 等非法资源引用导致报错的问题，并在迁移时级联清理遗留 V1 应用及其关联版本、对话和外链。 41. 修复搜索词高亮组件（`HighlightText`）直接使用未转义的搜索词构造正则表达式，导致输入括号 `(`、`[`、`c++` 等特殊字符时页面崩溃的问题。 42. 修复网页链接导入知识库时未补全相对路径的图片与链接（如 `./`、`../` 等），导致导入后图片无法显示、链接失效的问题。 43. 修复 OpenAI 兼容接口合并历史消息时多条 system 消息顺序被颠倒的问题，确保按原始相对顺序传递给模型。 44. 修复 Markdown 链接安全校验时，包含非 UTF-8 百分号编码（如 GBK 编码查询参数）的合法链接被误判为不安全链接导致无法点击、图片无法显示的问题。 45. 修复文档解析（如 Docx、XLSX 保留的 HTML 表格）中内嵌的 HTML `<img>` base64 图片未被自动上传存储、残留巨大 base64 字符串至分块中的问题。 46. 修复工作流条件判断器（`If/Else`）对非字符串值使用「开始为」或「结束为」条件时报 `trim is not a function` 导致节点运行报错的问题。 47. 修复部分知识库文件导入入口未将 S3 对象 key 绑定到已鉴权资源的问题。 48. 修复配置外部文档解析服务且开启 xlsx 解析时，知识库模板和备份导入因路由到外部解析（仅返回 Markdown）导致结构化表头与表格校验失败报「文件格式错误」的问题；模板与备份导入强制使用系统内置解析。  ## 🛠️ 代码优化  1. 模型测试接口支持不配置 channel 即可请求。 2. 应用和文件夹新增置顶接口，列表接口可按需启用置顶排序。 3. 知识库接口支持外部文档解析配置，可控制页眉页脚、附录、图片识别和图表解析结果的保留；仅在启用外部解析服务时生效。 4. 重构定时任务与分布式锁架构（`checkTimerLock`），模型状态探测核心逻辑内聚于 service 层，避免多节点并发探测。 5. 优化系统敏感数据加解密性能，缓存 scrypt 派生密钥并移除阻塞式的 CPU 信息查询；主应用 Dockerfile 基础镜像切换至 debian-slim。 6. 升级 hono、multer、nodemailer 等核心依赖版本。  ## What's Changed * ci: allow manual release tags by @c121914yu in https://github.com/labring/FastGPT/pull/7729 * fix(admin): require license ### 2026-09-30T13:09:14Z [**QuantumNous/new-api**](https://github.com/QuantumNous/new-api)
```

```
> [!IMPORTANT] > **Access Tokens & Task Plugins / 访问令牌与任务插件** > > 安全与访问里的单个面板访问令牌，改为最多 20 个具名访问令牌。创建时只显示一次，默认 30 天过期；作用域按本人已有权限勾选。升级后旧版令牌还能再用 30 天，之后会被拒绝，且不能重新生成，请在到期前换成新令牌。 > > 管理员管理用户时，删除、改状态或角色、设置密码或权限、创建管理员、重置通行密钥、关闭两步验证、清除绑定，都需要一次性校验。额度调整，以及修改显示名、分组、备注，不需要。 > > 任务插件改由 moejs 运行，性能大幅提升，插件接口不变。 > > 豆包 Seedream 图片改走任务插件的 OpenAI Images（`/v1/images/generations`、`/v1/images/edits`）。新增 Seedream 5.0 Flash；各模型按自己的档位计价。任务插件收窄用量项之后，已保存的价格仍可编辑。 > > Security & Access replaces the single dashboard Access Token with up to 20 named Access tokens. A token is shown once at creation, expires in 30 days by default, and its Scopes stay within what the owner can already do. The Legacy token keeps working for 30 days after the upgrade, then it is rejected and cannot be regenerated. Replace it before then. > > Admin user management now requires a single-use verification for delete, status or role changes, password or Permissions, creating an admin, resetting a Passkey, disabling 2FA, and clearing a binding. Quota adjustments, and edits to display name, Group, or remark, do not. > > Task Plugins now run on moejs, with a large performance improvement.  > > Doubao Seedream images now go through the Task Plugin OpenAI Images paths (`/v1/images/generations`, `/v1/images/edits`). Seedream 5.0 Flash is added, and each model is priced on its own tiers. Prices saved before a Task Plugin narrows its usage facts stay editable.  ## New Features  * Replaced the single dashboard Access Token with up to 20 named Access tokens on Security & Access. Tokens are shown once, use Scopes, and expire after 30 days by default. * Access tokens can be renamed without verification. Changing Permissions requires a single-use proof bound to that token. * Moved Task Plugins to moejs, with a large performance improvement. The plugin API is unchanged. * Added Seedream 5.0 Flash, and price each Seedream model on its own tiers. Seedream images are served through the Task Plugin OpenAI Images protocol. * Highlight differing values when comparing source price expressions ([#7543](https://github.com/QuantumNous/new-api/issues/7543)). * Added a Refresh button on the Channels table.  ## Fixes and Improvements  * Keep Pricing Configuration editable after a usage profile drops facts. Remaining prices stay in place. * Keep Responses custom tools when converting to Chat Completions, so tools such as `exec` and `apply_patch` are no longer dropped. * Preserve Claude auto-mode safeguards and per-message `output_config` ([#7598](https://github.com/QuantumNous/new-api/issues/7598), [#7561](https://github.com/QuantumNous/new-api/issues/7561)). * Preserve the Responses WebSocket setting when saving a Channel ([#7468](https://github.com/QuantumNous/new-api/issues/7468)). * Revalidate Marketplace downloads so a republished plugin source does not fail the integrity check from a stale cache. * Reject non-standard roles when creating a user. * Release binaries now embed the real version instead of `v0.0.0` ([#7536](https://github.com/QuantumNous/new-api/issues/7536)).  ## What's Changed * test(billing): fix websocket rejection refund test after pre-consume dropped output estimation by @feitianbubu in https://github.com/QuantumNous/new-api/pull/7491 * fix(plugins/alibaba): keep upstream metadata in openai_image render by @feitianbubu in https://github.com/QuantumNous/new-api/pull/7507 * fix(relayconvert): Responses→Chat 工具输出图片改为提升到后续 user 消息 by @Calcium-Ion in https://github.com/QuantumNous/new-api/pull/7510 * fix(relayconvert): Claude tool_result 图片提升到 user 消息；Chat→Responses 流补齐 reasoning part 事件并按段切分 item by @Calcium-Ion in https://github.com/QuantumNous/new-api/pull/7512 * fix(logs): keep model badge text readable and compact by @Yorick-Ryu in https://github.com/QuantumNous/new-api/pull/7504 * fix: move force operation into confirm slot so plugin-in-use dialog has one cancel button by @feitianbubu in https://github.com/QuantumNous/new-api/pull/7528 * fix(web): keep text selection when opening a model pricing row for editing by @RedwindA in https://github.com/QuantumNous/new-api/pull/7403 * fix(ci): align ldflags -X package path with module path so release binaries embed the real version by @mslchy in https://github.com/QuantumNous/new-api/pull/7536 * fix: match current price column width to source columns so expressions wrap alike by @feitianbubu in https://github.com/QuantumNous/new-api/pull/7542 * feat: highlight differing values in source price expressions by @feitianbubu in https://github.com/QuantumNous/new-api/pull/7543 * style(playground): add break-all to playground input by @LauZzL in https://github.com/QuantumNous/new-api/pull/6600 * fix(channel): preserve Responses WebSocket setting on save by @zenor0 in https://github.com/QuantumNous/new-api/pull/7468 * fix(claude): preserve per-message output_config in Claude messages by @Zane-0x5a in https://github.com/QuantumNous/new-api/pull/7561 * fix(claude): preserve auto mode safeguards in native requests by @seefs001 in https://github.com/QuantumNous/new-api/pull/7598  ## New ## v2.5.7  <details> <summary><strong> 🐞 修复问题 </strong></summary>  - 修复读屏软件将普通模式下的侧边栏导航项播报为不可用的可拖拽控件的问题 - 修复订阅包含自定义 DNS 时，确认开启的 DNS 覆写在重启应用后被自动关闭的问题  **🖥️ Windows**  - 修复 Windows 系统盘根目录的删除权限被误判，导致服务模式和 TUN 无法使用的问题 - 修复 Windows 部分系统安装服务失败，提示「program not found」的问题  **🍎 macOS**  - macOS 修复启动或重启应用时偶发内核启动失败，并误提示「需要更新系统服务」的问题 - macOS 修复 VPN 接管网络或开机网络未就绪时的问题：服务模式内核无法启动、TUN 不可用、系统代理状态读取报错 - macOS 修复用 `ipconfig set` 手动配置网卡后无法设置系统代理的问题  **🍎/🐧 macOS/Linux**  - 修复 macOS/Linux 服务模式停止或重启内核时直接强制结束的问题：内核来不及正常退出、最后的日志丢失  </details>  <details> <summary><strong> 🚀 优化改进 </strong></summary>  - 优化服务模式的问题排查：服务自身的运行日志会保存到文件，便于追查内核被停止的原因  **🖥️ Windows**  - 优化 Windows 服务安全检查未通过时的启动提示：不再只显示「服务无法启动内核」，直接说明原因并提供修复文档链接  </details>  ## 下载地址  ### Windows (不再支持Win7) #### 正常版本(推荐) - ### 2026-10-06T19:25:58Z [**tracel-ai/burn**](https://github.com/tracel-ai/burn)
```

```
# Summary  Burn `0.22.0` brings five months of improvements to training and inference, along with our biggest API update yet. Over the last four years, we've kept APIs stable where possible while collecting your feedback on what could be improved. This release brings Burn's API much closer to what we want for 1.0.  The biggest change is that models and tensors no longer carry a backend generic. Devices select where and how they run, and editing a model requires much less recompilation. The release also adds LoRA and QLoRA support, parameter groups and multiple optimizers, graph capture, and improvements to remote execution. New tensor operations, optimizers, and kernel improvements round out the release.  For more details, [check out the release post on our website](https://tracel.ai/blog/release-0.22.0).  ## No More `B: Backend`  Burn is still built around the `Backend` trait, with composable support for autodiff, kernel fusion, and remote execution. User code now selects these capabilities through `Device`, while models are plain types:  rust use burn::{     module::Module,     nn::{Linear, LinearConfig},     tensor::Device, };  #[derive(Module, Debug)] pub struct Model {     linear: Linear, }  // Requires the cuda feature. Other options include Device::wgpu(..) and Device::flex(). let device = Device::cuda(0); let model = Model {     linear: LinearConfig::new(4, 2).init(&device), };   More of the infrastructure is now implemented in Rust: Pliron replaces the MLIR-based intermediate representation, and Turso replaces the bundled SQLite dependency. CubeCL also adds LLVM-based CUDA and AMD GPU compilation paths. These changes reduce reliance on bundled C and C++ components and make more of the stack accessible to Rust contributors, though LLVM remains a native dependency for the backends that use it. Clean builds can take longer even as model-edit rebuilds become much faster.  # Changelog  **Breaking changes and migration**  See [Migrating to Burn 0.22 in the Burn Book](https://burn.dev/books/burn/migrating-to-0.22.html) for the complete migration guide, including before-and-after examples for devices, autodiff, modules, training, datasets, tensor operations, and custom integrations.  Start by removing backend parameters such as `Model<B>` and `Tensor<B, D>`, selecting a `Device`, and enabling autodiff with `device.autodiff()` before initializing a training model and its inputs. Candle has been removed in this release, and NdArray and LibTorch are being deprecated.  Records now use burnpack. Legacy MessagePack, binary, and JSON recorder files must be loaded with an older compatible Burn version and exported through `burn-store` before importing the weights into 0.22. Follow the [checkpoint migration instructions](https://github.com/tracel-ai/burn/blob/main/burn-book/src/migrating-to-0.22.md#migrating-checkpoints); this transfers model parameters, not legacy optimizer or scheduler state.  ## What's Changed  ### Tensor & Modules  - [Breaking] Remove `Tensor` backend generic and add high-level `Device` struct (#4717) @laggui - Refactor tensor kind traits (#4957) @laggui - refactor(tensor): add `BridgeTensor` to bridge high-level tensor API with dispatch (#4958) @laggui - Bool tensor API improvements (#4955) @Cielbird - fix(nn): conv1d initializer fan out (#4973) @laggui - feat(ops): autodiff for rfft/irfft (#4956) @cofinite - feat: add `Calibration::AbsMean` for BitNet b1.58 ternary weight quantization (#4989) @simeon-kepp - refactor(tensor)!: replace `TensorKind::id()` with `const KIND` and rename `TensorKindId` -> `Kind` (#5018) @laggui - refactor(tensor): seal `TensorKind` and `Parameter` traits and consolidate param impls (#5022) @laggui - fix(module): clearer error when generic used in both module and skip fields (#5028) @jaweed3 - fix(tensor): detect NaN element-wise in contains_nan to avoid f16 false positives (#5027) @zhan-wei-919 - fix(quantization): fallback (dequant -> op -> quant) implementation for slice, gather, select and expand (#5020) @Andy2887 - feat(nn): add rotary encoding reset (#5042) @nicolaslara - feat(module): add Identity Module, extend Activation and Normalization. (#5058) @crutcher - feat(nn): add MultiHeadAttention configuration flags for Linear::bias. (#5059) @crutcher - fix(param): cloning shares lazy initialization state (#5045) @laggui - fix(tensor): prevent unit dim strides from corrupting `split_strides` output (#5071) @ArthurBrussee - feat(tensor): hypot function (#5065) @skewballfox - feat(module): param group (#5086) @Charles23R - fix(tensor): resolve unsupported bool storage to device default (#5094) @yasumorishima - feat(autodiff): implement prod and prod_dim (#5106) @leonardwetzel - Implement int_scatter_nd for autodiff backend (#5114) @weiji14 - Feat/lora qlora (#5139) @Charles23R - Feat/spread param groups (#5154) @Charles23R - LstmState: Clone, Debug, initial, shape, stack, slice, (un)squeeze (#5167) @crutcher - Feat/relu6 activation (#5143) @Bellman281 - feat: add hardtanh and tanhshrink activation functions (#5144) @Bellman281 - feat: add threshold activation function (#5145) @Bellman281 - feat: add margin ranking loss (#5151) @Bellman281 - feat: add HingeEmbeddingLoss (#5174) @Bellman281 - feat: add soft margin loss (#5149) @Bellman281 - feat: add RReLU activation module (#5150) @Bellman281 - feat: add Mish, SiLU and LogSigmoid activation modules (#5183) @Bellman281 - fix: compute margin^p without std-only f64::powi (no_std build) (#5176) @Bellman281 - Add LstmState::chunk method (#5178) @jaweed3 - feat: add CosineSimilarity module (#5186) @Bellman281 - feat: add PairwiseDistance module (#5185) @Bellman281 - feat: add TripletMarginLoss (#5175) @Bellman281 - feat: add GaussianNLLLoss (#5182) @Bellman281 - feat: add PixelShuffle and PixelUnshuffle modules (#5184) @Bellman281 - feat: support negative GLU dimensions (#5203) @mattisonchao - fix(autodiff): zero-safe cumprod gradient (#5141) @AnayGarodia - feat: support negative softmax dimensions (#5205) @mattisonchao - Fix norm accumulation (#5211) @nathanielsimard - feat: support negative cumulative dimensions (#5213) @mattisonchao - feat: support negative FFT dimensions (#5214) @mattisonchao - feat: support negative sort and top-k dimensions (#5215) @mattisonchao - feat: support negative dimensions in statistics and norms (#5225) @mattisonchao - feat: Tensor<D, Int>::square() support (#5224) @crutcher - feat: support negative dimensions across tensor APIs (#5226) @mattisonchao - fix(autodiff): fix deadlock when a Backward step runs a nested backward (#5194) @laurigates - refactor(tensor): make `_elem` comparison methods aliases to unify `_scalar` methods (#5230) @laggui - fix(tensor): preserve dimension check errors (#5232) @mattisonchao - feat(tensor): add mask_select (boolean mask indexing) default impl (#5238) @Yashiru - ## v4.15 Win10/11 预打包版本下载  <details> <summary>MacOS/Linux系统请使用源码部署</summary>  1. 安装依赖：    bash    # macOS    brew install libsndfile git        brew tap homebrew-ffmpeg/ffmpeg 	    brew install homebrew-ffmpeg/ffmpeg/ffmpeg        # Ubuntu/Debian    sudo apt-get install ffmpeg libsndfile1-dev      3. 安装 uv：    bash    curl -LsSf https://astral.sh/uv/install.sh | sh      4. 克隆并启动：    bash    git clone https://github.com/jianchang512/pyvideotrans.git    cd pyvideotrans    uv sync    uv run sp.py      </details>   ##  更新日志/Changelos  - 更新 Deepgram.com 渠道模型并增加语音合成支持 - 更新 Elevenlabs.io 模型并移除将被废弃的模型 - 增加 `菜单-工具-高级选项-字幕配音-移除每条字幕配音全部静音缓冲`和`静音移除力度`可调节移除更多或更少静音 - 修复已知bug  ### What's Changed * feat(recognition): add Audexum speech-to-text channel by @audexum in https://github.com/jianchang512/pyvideotrans/pull/1216 * feat(translator): add Infistar translation channel by @lx7738 in https://github.com/jianchang512/pyvideotrans/pull/1217 * feat(language): add Azerbaijani to main language codes by @pateti-hub in https://github.com/jianchang512/pyvideotrans/pull/1219  ### New Contributors * @audexum made their first contribution in https://github.com/jianchang512/pyvideotrans/pull/1216 * @lx7738 made their first contribution in https://github.com/jianchang512/pyvideotrans/pull/1217 * @pateti-hub made their first contribution in https://github.com/jianchang512/pyvideotrans/pull/1219  **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/jianchang512/pyvideotrans/compare/v4.14...v4.15   ----  ### v4.15 完整包下载(2.7G)  - ### 2026-10-01T13:43:37Z [**lollipopkit/flutter_server_box**](https://github.com/lollipopkit/flutter_server_box)
```

```
### Before you upgrade  - **Previous Agent conversations are removed.** The new Agent engine cannot use the old conversation history, which was not included in backups. - **Some settings have been removed**, including the old CPU and network display options, server history, card ordering, custom theme catalog URL, and opening terminals in the system SSH app. Terminals now always open in ServerBox. - **Some Monitor features require agent 0.2.0**, including accounts and roles, port forwarding, longer history and Monitor settings. When upgraded, existing accounts become admins with permissions based on the old access settings.  ### Added  - **Remote desktop:** Connect to servers with RDP or VNC through SSH or a Monitor agent, without exposing the desktop port. Multiple connection profiles are supported. Password saving is optional; audio, multiple monitors and file transfer are not supported. - **Virtualization:** Manage Proxmox VE and libvirt/KVM virtual machines and containers, including power, console, snapshots, storage and networks. Proxmox settings are carried over from the server page. - **Use this device as a server** on Linux, Windows and the macOS DMG build, with status, processes, services, containers, terminal and files. - **Themes and a theme store:** Install official themes or import `.fsbt` packages. Existing AMOLED modes are converted to the AMOLED theme. - **Port forwarding** through a Monitor agent, including local, remote and SOCKS5 forwarding. SSH also supports SOCKS5 forwarding. - **Monitor accounts, roles and settings** can be managed in the app, including access permissions, collection, alerts, data retention and notifications. - **`serverbox://` links** can open servers, features and snippets or prefill a new server. Links do not include passwords or keys, and snippets ask before running. - **ProxyCommand on Android and iOS**, plus access to installed Linux files from Android's file picker. - **More monitoring data**, including GPU, disk, network, battery, sensor and SMART readings, with up to 30 days of agent history. - **App-drawn tmux controls, reduced motion, improved terminal reconnection** and support for older SSH servers are also available.  ### Changed  - **Server and settings pages have a new layout**, with searchable settings, server search and sorting, clearer status information and a more compact phone navigation bar. - **Server connections are easier to manage:** SSH and Monitor can be enabled separately and ordered as preferred or fallback connections. - **The Agent has a new engine** with provider and tool choices, separate app and terminal conversations, image attachments and optional automatic approval for commands judged safe. - **Server management pages are improved** for containers, users, processes, services and scheduled tasks. Services can be managed without opening a terminal, and process and task lists are easier to inspect. - Files in the old device Documents location are copied to the new app storage location; the originals remain in place.  ### Fixed  - Fixed process controls and status readings on Linux, Windows, macOS and BSD. - Fixed user and scheduled task pages on servers using fish or BusyBox. - Fixed backup restore and import issues, including duplicate server names and older keys; server renames now keep benchmark history. - Fixed settings sync, server sorting, SSH connection cleanup and several crashes caused by unexpected server responses. - Fixed Monitor agent hangs and alert rules that missed data or did not run.   中文更新日志：https://serverbox.lollipopkit.com/docs/zh/changelog/  **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/lollipopkit/flutter_server_box/compare/v1.0.1617...v1.0.1719 
```

### 2026-10-07T05:04:03Z [**klzgrad/naiveproxy**](https://github.com/klzgrad/naiveproxy)

```
Improved QUIC throughput to near TLS performance with higher receive window and BBR congestion control. Users are recommended to update Caddy server to [v2.11.7-naive](https://github.com/klzgrad/forwardproxy/releases/tag/v2.11.7-naive).  Softened old tunnel cleanup that breaks long-lived user connections. Now in old tunnels, long-lived connections are only closed after 1/10 idle timeout. 
```

### 2026-10-05T04:39:57Z [**tw93/Pake**](https://github.com/tw93/Pake)

```
<div align="center"> <img src="https://gw.alipayobjects.com/zos/k/fa/logo-modified.png" alt="Pake Logo" width="120" height="120" style="border-radius:50%" /> <h1 style="margin: 12px 0 6px;">Pake V3.17.3</h1> <p><em>Turn any webpage into a desktop app with one command.</em></p> </div>  ### Changelog  1. Native capabilities such as notifications, downloads, zoom, window controls, and opening links in the system browser are now granted only to local pages and the exact origin of the packaged URL, so a page that navigates to another site can no longer call them. Package the final URL a site lands on, for example `https://mail.google.com` rather than `https://gmail.com`. 2. Clicking a notification on macOS brings back the window that sent it and removes it from Notification Center, and with `--multi-window` a message raised by several windows is shown once and counted once on the Dock badge. 3. Downloads send the page's User-Agent, Referer, and session cookies, including after redirects, so files behind a login save correctly. A failed download no longer leaves a truncated file, and simultaneous downloads with the same name are saved separately. 4. Linux apps default to a User-Agent that matches their WebKit engine instead of claiming to be Chrome, which lets YouTube captions load. `--user-agent` still overrides it. 5. Linux apps follow the desktop's light or dark preference and switch live when it changes, while `--dark-mode` still takes priority. 6. On macOS, `window.print()` opens the print dialog, two-finger trackpad swipes go back and forward, and the tab bar shows a + button when `--multi-window` is on. 7. Windows adds the opt-in `--password-autosave`, which offers to save passwords in the app's own WebView2 profile and stays off in private windows. `--windows-toolchain gnu --keep-binary` now places `WebView2Loader.dll` beside the executable so it starts on its own.  ### 更新日志  1. 通知、下载、缩放、窗口控制和用系统浏览器打开链接这些原生能力，现在只开放给本地页面和打包网址的精确来源，页面跳到其他站点后就无法再调用，打包时要用网站最终打开的网址，比如 `https://mail.google.com` 而不是 `https://gmail.com`。 2. macOS 上点击通知会唤回发出它的窗口并从通知中心移除，开启 `--multi-window` 时多个窗口收到的同一条消息只弹一次，程序坞角标也只加一次。 3. 下载会带上页面的 User-Agent、Referer 和登录 Cookie，跳转之后也不会丢，需要登录的文件可以正常保存，下载失败不再留下不完整的文件，同名文件同时下载时也会分别保存。 4. Linux 应用默认使用与 WebKit 引擎一致的 User-Agent，不再自称 Chrome，YouTube 字幕因此可以正常加载，`--user-agent` 依然可以覆盖。 5. Linux 应用会跟随桌面的浅色或深色偏好，运行中切换也会实时生效，`--dark-mode` 仍然优先。 6. macOS 上 `window.print()` 可以弹出打印面板，触控板双指左右滑动可以前进和后退，开启 `--multi-window` 时标签栏会显示 + 按钮。 7. Windows 新增可选的 `--password-autosave`，开启后应用会在自己的 WebView2 配置里提示保存密码，隐私窗口中始终关闭，`--windows-toolchain gnu --keep-binary` 也会把 `WebView2Loader.dll` 放在可执行文件旁边，程序可以直接启动。  Special thanks to @Yaman-Harfoush, @nhanntgit, @karl007, and @BenzidaneMo for the detailed reports, and to @yhcharles, @davidscottpope-gif, @WavesMan, @BenzidaneMo, and @jakero for the pull requests behind this release. If Pake helps you, please consider giving it a star and recommending it to your friends.  > https://github.com/tw93/Pake 
```

### 2026-09-30T12:39:54Z [**m1k1o/neko**](https://github.com/m1k1o/neko)

```
### New Features - Added a capture subscription API to support more transports ([#699](https://github.com/m1k1o/neko/pull/699)). - Added support for restricting OAuth login by user email, along with a new `email` OAuth scope. - Added OIDC-only member authentication ([#686](https://github.com/m1k1o/neko/pull/686)). - Enabled automatic video quality (bandwidth estimation) for legacy (v2) clients ([#693](https://github.com/m1k1o/neko/pull/693)). - Added a mouse pointer visibility toggle via `capture.video.show_pointer` ([#691](https://github.com/m1k1o/neko/pull/691)). - Added multi-file selection and bulk deletion to file transfer ([#687](https://github.com/m1k1o/neko/pull/687)). - Added file deletion support and a user delete config option to file transfer ([#683](https://github.com/m1k1o/neko/pull/683)). - Added a `?scroll` query param to set scroll sensitivity on load ([#681](https://github.com/m1k1o/neko/pull/681)). - Added an arm64 Google Chrome image ([#685](https://github.com/m1k1o/neko/pull/685)). - Added example Docker Compose configurations for simple browser, TURN server, GPU-accelerated browser, and OAuth setups ([#684](https://github.com/m1k1o/neko/pull/684)). - Added an "open in app" plugin to open chat links in a shared neko instance ([#682](https://github.com/m1k1o/neko/pull/682)).  ### Fixes - Fixed missing clipboard fallback button in Safari ([#709](https://github.com/m1k1o/neko/pull/709)). - Fixed a XSS vulnerability by sanitizing attribute values and preventing Vue template compilation in rendered markdown. - Fixed video pipeline configuration to accept a bare pipeline string ([#701](https://github.com/m1k1o/neko/pull/701)). - Fixed a goroutine leak in the RTCP PLI ticker ([#697](https://github.com/m1k1o/neko/pull/697)). - Fixed a memory leak by freeing `CString` allocations and `GFile` references in drag URI handling ([#700](https://github.com/m1k1o/neko/pull/700)). - Fixed a nil pointer panic when destroying the WebRTC peer during a profile change ([#695](https://github.com/m1k1o/neko/pull/695)). - Fixed file transfer to allow non-admin uploads and downloads ([#679](https://github.com/m1k1o/neko/pull/679)). - Fixed the TOR browser image to fetch it from the latest download source.  ### Misc - Removed the default configuration file bundled with the image; defaults are now built into the server, preventing accidental overrides when providing a custom config. - Updated persistent data policies for Firefox-based browsers. 
```

### 2026-10-01T11:13:34Z [**PowerJob/PowerJob**](https://github.com/PowerJob/PowerJob)

```
PowerJob 5.1.7 is a small functional bugfix release.  ## Bug fixes  - Fix the first DAILY_TIME_INTERVAL trigger before a daily window so that it starts at the window boundary. - Preserve Job definitions when creating Workflow child instances, and restore their Workflow scheduling metadata after a cold load. - Resolve application types in SpEL with a fallback class loader while preserving the existing context loader behavior. - Prevent late MU connection-close callbacks from removing replacement connections, and complete malformed-response futures exceptionally. - Avoid an extra unlock exception when Worker lock acquisition is interrupted; safely close a Remote Engine that has not initialized. - Forward Docker shutdown signals to the JVM. - Fix Console instance navigation, Job list filter pagination and stale responses, copied date ranges, DAILY expression editing, and Workflow editor refresh.  ## Upgrade compatibility  Java 8, existing protocols, authentication behavior, configuration, and database schema remain unchanged; no schema migration is required. Existing Client and Worker versions can continue using an upgraded Server. To receive MU transport or Worker-local fixes, upgrade the affected component. The Server distribution includes the paired Console fixes.  [Changes since 5.1.6](https://github.com/PowerJob/PowerJob/compare/v5.1.6...v5.1.7) 
```

### 2026-10-04T12:10:56Z [**huginn/huginn**](https://github.com/huginn/huginn)

```
# v2026.10.04  ## Changes  - Upgrade to json 3.0.2, which rejects duplicate object keys and JavaScript-style comments by default. - Deduplicate automatic Agent checks from schedules and controller Agents while a check is queued, running, or awaiting retry.  [#3767](https://github.com/huginn/huginn/pull/3767) - Move navigation labels to translation keys while preserving the English text.  [#3763](https://github.com/huginn/huginn/pull/3763) - Upgrade the bundled database and Docker Compose MySQL services to MySQL 8.4 LTS, with bundled server support on amd64 and arm64.  [#3757](https://github.com/huginn/huginn/pull/3757)  ## Upgrading  Check external JSON inputs for duplicate keys and comments, and review custom Agent gems for json 3 API compatibility.  The gem update requires no database migration.  See the [JSON upgrade notes](https://github.com/huginn/huginn/blob/v2026.10.04/UPGRADING.md#stricter-json-parsing-with-json-3).  Before upgrading a bundled or Docker Compose MySQL database, back it up and follow the [MySQL upgrade guide](https://github.com/huginn/huginn/blob/v2026.10.04/UPGRADING.md#moving-official-images-to-mysql-8).  MySQL 5.7 requires an intermediate upgrade to 8.0 before 8.4.  **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/huginn/huginn/compare/v2026.09.22...v2026.10.04 
```

```
* [Milestone](https://github.com/FreshRSS/FreshRSS/milestones/1.30.1)  This is a security-oriented release with several security patches and bug fixes in the continuation of [1.30.0](https://github.com/FreshRSS/FreshRSS/releases/tag/1.30.0).  Note that our rolling-release channel (*edge*) is recommended for faster security patches.  Bug fixes highlights 🐛: * Some security attack scenarios patched * Fix domain-wide `Retry-After` * Fix muting of feeds gone with HTTP 410  Feature highlights✨: * CLI: Add optional feed argument to `actualize-user.php`  This release has been made by @Alkarex, @andris155, @fzlzjerry, @Inverle, @jamalkamaladdin, @jtracey, @Otolock and newcomers @akine, @colons, @ethanstoner, @gigioneggiando, @Juice-de-Orange, @pavel-miniutka, @qwist1233-cpu, @Sharawey74, @shcheglovnd, @TapuGithub, @yuchenshi, @ZainnQureshii  Full [changelog](https://github.com/FreshRSS/FreshRSS/blob/1.30.1/CHANGELOG.md):  * Deployment 	* Reword recommendations and explanations for *edge* (rolling release) vs. *latest* (versioned release) channels [#9270](https://github.com/FreshRSS/FreshRSS/pull/9270) * Security 	* Config + increase default values for search max length and depth [#9280](https://github.com/FreshRSS/FreshRSS/pull/9280) 	* Accept a trusted proxy address given without a subnet [#9301](https://github.com/FreshRSS/FreshRSS/pull/9301) 	* Warn during session regenerate fail [#9311](https://github.com/FreshRSS/FreshRSS/pull/9311), [#9376](https://github.com/FreshRSS/FreshRSS/pull/9376) 	* CI/CD add zizmor workflow for action security checks [#9228](https://github.com/FreshRSS/FreshRSS/pull/9228), [#9331](https://github.com/FreshRSS/FreshRSS/pull/9331) 	* Use PHP `#[\SensitiveParameter]` [#9322](https://github.com/FreshRSS/FreshRSS/pull/9322) 	* Reject token access (RSS/OPML export, feed refresh) for disabled accounts [#9336](https://github.com/FreshRSS/FreshRSS/pull/9336) 	* Require POST+CSRF for self-update mutations [#9335](https://github.com/FreshRSS/FreshRSS/pull/9335) 	* Make the login challenge nonce one-time [#9334](https://github.com/FreshRSS/FreshRSS/pull/9334) 	* Bound ZIP import against decompression bombs [#9332](https://github.com/FreshRSS/FreshRSS/pull/9332) 	* Rotate session ID on all authenticated transitions [#9333](https://github.com/FreshRSS/FreshRSS/pull/9333) 	* Minz: Remove vulnerable and unused code path for displaying errors [#9395](https://github.com/FreshRSS/FreshRSS/pull/9395) 	* Fix NAT64 feed fetching on IPv6-only hosts [#9372](https://github.com/FreshRSS/FreshRSS/pull/9372) * Bug fixes 	* Fix domain-wide `Retry-After` [#9390](https://github.com/FreshRSS/FreshRSS/pull/9390) 	* Fix muting of feeds gone with HTTP 410 [#9391](https://github.com/FreshRSS/FreshRSS/pull/9391) 	* Fix infinite redirect loop due to `SCRIPT_NAME` in `PATH_INFO` [#9282](https://github.com/FreshRSS/FreshRSS/pull/9282), [#9287](https://github.com/FreshRSS/FreshRSS/pull/9287) 	* Restore the automatic reading view after marking articles as read, while preserving explicit filters [#9288](https://github.com/FreshRSS/FreshRSS/issues/9288) 	* Fix adaptive reading state after marking articles read [#9290](https://github.com/FreshRSS/FreshRSS/pull/9290) 	* Fix regression sharing links [#9303](https://github.com/FreshRSS/FreshRSS/pull/9303) 	* Don’t disable anonymous refresh when anonymous feed access is disabled [#9354](https://github.com/FreshRSS/FreshRSS/pull/9354) 	* Fix regression with HTTPS proxies due to wrong TLS SNI resolution [#9341](https://github.com/FreshRSS/FreshRSS/pull/9341) 	* Better enforce limits for feeds and categories [#9357](https://github.com/FreshRSS/FreshRSS/pull/9357) 	* Fix JavaScript error when opening a label menu in sidebar [#9377](https://github.com/FreshRSS/FreshRSS/pull/9377) 	* Fix custom favicon with GReader API [#9409](https://github.com/FreshRSS/FreshRSS/pull/9409) * CLI 	* Add optional feed argument to `actualize-user.php` [#9319](https://github.com/FreshRSS/FreshRSS/pull/9319) * UI 	* Various UI and style improvements [#9298](https://github.com/FreshRSS/FreshRSS/pull/9298),
```
